 Editor performance log
docker exec ontocode-editor cat logs/owl-editor-performance.log
docker exec ontocode-editor tail -200 logs/owl-editor-performance.log

# SWRL performance log
docker exec ontocode-swrl cat logs/swrl-service-performance.log
docker exec ontocode-swrl tail -200 logs/swrl-service-performance.log

# Filter slow requests
docker exec ontocode-editor grep -E "[0-9]{4,}ms" logs/owl-editor-performance.log

# Copy to host
docker cp ontocode-editor:/app/logs/owl-editor-performance.log ./editor-perf.log
docker cp ontocode-swrl:/app/logs/swrl-service-performance.log ./swrl-perf.log
